## Results (post-election), PM edition

Source tables: House https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_United_States_House_of_Representatives_elections ; Senate https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_United_States_Senate_elections . Wikipedia editors mark a race called (Y winner mark or an outcome line) when AP or the networks call it. Treat a table call as 'reported called'; for any race you write up, cite a news link that confirms the call when one is in the leads below. Previous snapshot: 2026-11-04-am. Pre-election ratings frozen from 2026-11-03.

### Chamber status
- House: 375 of 435 called: D 235, R 140; 60 not marked called. Majority is 218.
- House, adding races reported called in news but not yet in the table (4: D 2, R 2): D 237, R 142. Label these as reported calls with their sources.
- Senate: 33 of 35 races called. Seats secured including those not up this year: D 54, R 44 (34 D and 31 R not up). Majority is 51 (the vice president breaks 50-50 ties).
- Safe seats (final consensus Solid) not yet marked called in the table: 26 (2 of them reported called in news). These are usually table lag, not real uncertainty (none has a count within 5 points); give them as a count in the brief, not race by race. Keys: HOU-AZ-05, HOU-AZ-08, HOU-CA-04, HOU-CA-07, HOU-CA-11, HOU-CA-14, HOU-CA-23, HOU-CA-29, HOU-CA-40, HOU-CA-47, HOU-FL-26, HOU-LA-06, HOU-NC-06, HOU-NC-08, HOU-NC-10, HOU-OK-05, HOU-SC-02, HOU-TN-04, HOU-TN-07, HOU-TX-02, HOU-TX-05, HOU-TX-22, HOU-TX-32, HOU-UT-02, HOU-UT-04, HOU-WA-04
